5) Critical Zero Trust Constraints (Must Mention in Project)

Even with CRUD:

1. Context Enforcement (ABAC)

Access allowed only if:

Correct device
Correct location
Correct time
Assigned patient
2. Break-Glass Policy
Emergency override
Fully logged
Time-limited
3. Data Sensitivity Layers
Full PHI
Partial PHI
De-identified
4. No Hard Delete Rule
D = Soft delete only
Full audit trail required

4) Attribute-Based Constraints (Critical for ZTA)

Every permission above is NOT absolute. It is evaluated with:

Identity Attributes
Role
Department
Clearance level
Resource Attributes
Patient ownership
Sensitivity level (PHI tier)
Environment Attributes
Location (hospital / remote)
Device trust score
Time (shift hours)
Example Policy
ALLOW Doctor
IF:
  role = "Physician"
  AND patient.assigned = true
  AND device.trusted = true
  AND location = hospital

ER doctors
ICU staff

Justified
Auto-expiry

3) DB Connection (Tenant-Aware + RLS Binding)
src/db/index.ts
import { Pool } from "pg";

export const pool = new Pool({
  connectionString: process.env.DB_URL,
});

export const getTenantClient = async (tenantId: string) => {
  const client = await pool.connect();
  await client.query(`SET app.tenant_id = '${tenantId}'`);
  return client;
};
4) Auth Middleware (JWT + ZTA Context)
src/middleware/auth.ts
import jwt from "jsonwebtoken";
import { Request, Response, NextFunction } from "express";

export interface AuthRequest extends Request {
  user?: any;
}

export const authMiddleware = (req: AuthRequest, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
  const token = req.headers.authorization?.split(" ")[1];

  if (!token) return res.status(401).json({ error: "No token" });

  try {
    const decoded = jwt.verify(token, process.env.JWT_SECRET!);
    req.user = decoded;
    next();
  } catch {
    return res.status(403).json({ error: "Invalid token" });
  }
};
5) Tenant Middleware (CRITICAL)
src/middleware/tenant.ts
import { Response, NextFunction } from "express";
import { AuthRequest } from "./auth";

export const tenantMiddleware = (req: AuthRequest, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
  const tenantId = req.user?.tenant_id;

  if (!tenantId) {
    return res.status(400).json({ error: "Tenant missing" });
  }

  req.headers["x-tenant-id"] = tenantId;
  next();
};
6) ZTA Policy Middleware (ABAC Enforcement)
src/middleware/policy.ts
import { AuthRequest } from "./auth";
import { Response, NextFunction } from "express";

export const enforcePolicy = (action: string) => {
  return (req: AuthRequest, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
    const { role } = req.user;

    // Example policy
    if (action === "READ_PATIENT") {
      if (["Doctor", "Nurse", "Admin"].includes(role)) {
        return next();
      }
    }

    if (action === "CREATE_PATIENT") {
      if (["Admin", "Receptionist"].includes(role)) {
        return next();
      }
    }

    return res.status(403).json({ error: "Access denied" });
  };
};
7) Patient Repository (DB Layer)
src/modules/patient/patient.repository.ts
import { getTenantClient } from "../../db";

export const createPatient = async (tenantId: string, data: any) => {
  const client = await getTenantClient(tenantId);

  try {
    const result = await client.query(
      `INSERT INTO patients 
      (id, tenant_id, first_name, last_name, dob, gender)
      VALUES (gen_random_uuid(), $1, $2, $3, $4, $5)
      RETURNING *`,
      [tenantId, data.first_name, data.last_name, data.dob, data.gender]
    );

    return result.rows[0];
  } finally {
    client.release();
  }
};

export const getPatientById = async (tenantId: string, id: string) => {
  const client = await getTenantClient(tenantId);

  try {
    const result = await client.query(
      `SELECT * FROM patients WHERE id = $1`,
      [id]
    );

    return result.rows[0];
  } finally {
    client.release();
  }
};
8) Service Layer (Business Logic)
src/modules/patient/patient.service.ts
import * as repo from "./patient.repository";
import { publishEvent } from "../../events/eventBus";
import { logAudit } from "../../services/audit.service";

export const createPatientService = async (tenantId: string, user: any, data: any) => {
  const patient = await repo.createPatient(tenantId, data);

  await publishEvent("PATIENT_CREATED", {
    tenant_id: tenantId,
    patient_id: patient.id,
    actor_id: user.id,
  });

  await logAudit({
    tenant_id: tenantId,
    user_id: user.id,
    action: "CREATE_PATIENT",
    resource_id: patient.id,
  });

  return patient;
};
9) Controller Layer
src/modules/patient/patient.controller.ts
import { Request, Response } from "express";
import * as service from "./patient.service";

export const createPatient = async (req: any, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const tenantId = req.headers["x-tenant-id"];
    const patient = await service.createPatientService(
      tenantId,
      req.user,
      req.body
    );

    res.json(patient);
  } catch (err) {
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Failed to create patient" });
  }
};

export const getPatient = async (req: any, res: Response) => {
  const tenantId = req.headers["x-tenant-id"];
  const patient = await service.getPatientService(tenantId, req.params.id);

  res.json(patient);
};
10) Routes
src/modules/patient/patient.routes.ts
import express from "express";
import * as controller from "./patient.controller";
import { authMiddleware } from "../../middleware/auth";
import { tenantMiddleware } from "../../middleware/tenant";
import { enforcePolicy } from "../../middleware/policy";

const router = express.Router();

router.post(
  "/",
  authMiddleware,
  tenantMiddleware,
  enforcePolicy("CREATE_PATIENT"),
  controller.createPatient
);

router.get(
  "/:id",
  authMiddleware,
  tenantMiddleware,
  enforcePolicy("READ_PATIENT"),
  controller.getPatient
);

export default router;
11) Audit Service
src/services/audit.service.ts
import { pool } from "../db";

export const logAudit = async (data: any) => {
  await pool.query(
    `INSERT INTO audit_logs (tenant_id, user_id, action, resource_id)
     VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4)`,

1) Multi-Tenant Architecture (Shared DB Model)
Core Principle

Every row must be scoped by:

tenant_id UUID NOT NULL

                 + Row Level Security (RLS)
                 + Application Enforcement
                 + Encryption Segmentation
2) Updated Database Schema (Tenant-Aware)
patients
CREATE TABLE patients (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

    last_name VARCHAR(100),

    gender VARCHAR(10),

3) Row-Level Security (MANDATORY)
Enable RLS
ALTER TABLE patients ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
Policy
CREATE POLICY tenant_isolation_policy
ON patients
USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id')::UUID);
Runtime Binding

Every request sets:

SET app.tenant_id = 'tenant-uuid';
Result
Cross-tenant access = impossible at DB level
Even if app fails → DB protects data
4) API Layer (Tenant-Aware)
Middleware Injection

Every request must include:

{
  "tenant_id": "uuid",
  "user_id": "uuid",
  "role": "Doctor"
}
Backend Enforcement
if (!request.tenant_id) {
  throw new Error("Tenant context missing");
}
Query Example
SELECT * FROM patients
WHERE tenant_id = :tenant_id
AND id = :patient_id;
5) Tenant-Aware Authorization (ZTA + ABAC)
Policy Example
{
  "role": "Doctor",
  "tenant_id": "T1",
  "patient_tenant_id": "T1",
  "patient_assigned": true
}
Rule
ALLOW IF:
tenant_id == patient.tenant_id
AND role == Doctor
AND patient_assigned == true
Critical Constraint
Cross-tenant access = ALWAYS DENY
6) Encryption Strategy (Tenant-Specific Keys)
Key Model
Master Key (KMS)
   ↓
Tenant Key (per tenant)
   ↓
Data Encryption Key (per record)
Benefit
Data breach in one tenant → others unaffected
Enables tenant-level key rotation
7) Caching (Tenant-Isolated)
Redis Keys
patient:{tenant_id}:{patient_id}
access:{tenant_id}:{user_id}:{patient_id}
Rule
NEVER cache without tenant_id
8) Event Architecture (Tenant Scoped)
Event Example
{
  "event_type": "PATIENT_UPDATED",
  "tenant_id": "T1",
  "patient_id": "P1",
  "actor_id": "U1"
}
Topic Design
patient-events.{tenant_id}

OR

shared-topic + tenant filter
9) Search Index (Tenant Partitioning)
Elasticsearch Index
patients_tenant_T1
patients_tenant_T2

OR

{
  "tenant_id": "T1"
}
Query Filter
{
  "term": { "tenant_id": "T1" }
}
10) Audit Logging (Tenant-Safe)
{
  "tenant_id": "T1",
  "user_id": "U1",
  "action": "READ_PATIENT",
  "resource_id": "P1",
  "timestamp": "...",
  "ip": "...",
  "result": "SUCCESS"
}
Storage
Append-only
Immutable
Partitioned by tenant
11) Break-Glass (Tenant Controlled)
Rules
Only within same tenant
Requires justification
Logged per tenant
Token
{
  "tenant_id": "T1",
  "scope": "patient:P1",
  "expires_in": "15m"
}
12) Concurrency Control (Tenant-Aware)
UPDATE patients
SET ...
WHERE id = :id
AND tenant_id = :tenant_id
AND version = :version;
13) Data Leakage Prevention (CRITICAL)
Must enforce at ALL layers:
1. DB → RLS
2. API → tenant filter
3. Cache → tenant keys
4. Logs → tenant tagging
5. Search → tenant filter
14) Multi-Tenant Edge Cases
Case 1: Same patient in multiple hospitals

→ Separate records per tenant

Case 2: Shared care (rare)

→ Use:

cross-tenant consent + temporary access token
Case 3: Tenant deletion
Soft delete tenant
Revoke keys
Archive data

Index Strategy

ON patients(tenant_id, id);

PARTITION BY HASH (tenant_id);

Single DB cluster

Read replicas
Sharding (future)

import { getTenantClient } from "../../db";

export const createOrder = async (tenantId: string, data: any) => {
  const client = await getTenantClient(tenantId);

  try {
    const result = await client.query(
      `INSERT INTO orders 
      (id, tenant_id, patient_id, doctor_id, order_type, status)
      VALUES (gen_random_uuid(), $1, $2, $3, $4, 'CREATED')
      RETURNING *`,
      [tenantId, data.patient_id, data.doctor_id, data.order_type]
    );

    return result.rows[0];
  } finally {
    client.release();
  }
};
Service Layer
order.service.ts
import * as repo from "./order.repository";
import { publishEvent } from "../../events/eventBus";
import { logAudit } from "../../services/audit.service";

export const createOrderService = async (tenantId: string, user: any, data: any) => {
  const order = await repo.createOrder(tenantId, data);

  await publishEvent("ORDER_CREATED", {
    tenant_id: tenantId,
    order_id: order.id,
    patient_id: data.patient_id,
    actor_id: user.id,
  });

  await logAudit({
    tenant_id: tenantId,
    user_id: user.id,
    action: "CREATE_ORDER",
    resource_id: order.id,
  });

  return order;
};
Controller
export const createOrder = async (req: any, res: any) => {
  const tenantId = req.headers["x-tenant-id"];
  const order = await createOrderService(tenantId, req.user, req.body);

  res.json(order);
};
Routes
router.post(
  "/",
  authMiddleware,
  tenantMiddleware,
  enforcePolicy("CREATE_ORDER"),
  createOrder
);

4) File Upload Middleware
Install
npm install multer
src/middleware/upload.ts
import multer from "multer";
import path from "path";
import fs from "fs";

export const createStorage = (tenantId: string, studyId: string) => {
  const dir = path.join("storage", `tenant_${tenantId}`, "imaging", `study_${studyId}`);

  if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) {
    fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
  }

  return multer.diskStorage({
    destination: dir,
    filename: (req, file, cb) => {
      cb(null, `${Date.now()}-${file.originalname}`);
    },
  });
};
5) Upload Controller (Imaging)
diagnostic.controller.ts
import multer from "multer";
import { createStorage } from "../../middleware/upload";
import { saveImagingFile } from "./diagnostic.repository";

export const uploadImaging = async (req: any, res: any) => {
  const tenantId = req.headers["x-tenant-id"];
  const studyId = req.params.id;

  const storage = createStorage(tenantId, studyId);
  const upload = multer({ storage }).single("file");

  upload(req, res, async (err) => {
    if (err) return res.status(500).json({ error: "Upload failed" });

    const filePath = req.file.path;

    await saveImagingFile(tenantId, studyId, filePath);

    res.json({ message: "File uploaded", filePath });
  });
};
6) Repository Update
export const saveImagingFile = async (
  tenantId: string,
  studyId: string,
  filePath: string
) => {
  const client = await getTenantClient(tenantId);

  try {
    await client.query(
      `INSERT INTO imaging_files 
       (id, tenant_id, study_id, file_path)
       VALUES (gen_random_uuid(), $1, $2, $3)`,
      [tenantId, studyId, filePath]
    );
  } finally {
    client.release();
  }
};
7) Secure File Access (CRITICAL)
NEVER expose raw file paths directly
API Endpoint

Controller
import fs from "fs";

export const getImagingFile = async (req: any, res: any) => {
  const tenantId = req.headers["x-tenant-id"];
  const { fileId } = req.params;

  const file = await getFileMetadata(tenantId, fileId);

  if (!file) return res.status(404).send("Not found");

  // ZTA check (role + tenant)
  if (file.tenant_id !== tenantId) {
    return res.status(403).send("Forbidden");
  }

  res.sendFile(file.file_path, { root: process.cwd() });
};

export const verifyPrescription = async (tenantId: string, id: string, user: any) => {
  const client = await getTenantClient(tenantId);

  await client.query(
    `UPDATE prescriptions
     SET status = 'VERIFIED'
     WHERE id = $1`,
    [id]
  );
};
10) Dispensing Logic (CRITICAL)
Steps
Check stock
Allocate from batches (FIFO)
Reduce inventory
Record dispensing
Implementation
export const dispenseMedication = async (tenantId: string, prescriptionId: string, user: any) => {
  const client = await getTenantClient(tenantId);

  try {
    await client.query("BEGIN");

    const items = await client.query(
      `SELECT * FROM prescription_items WHERE prescription_id = $1`,
      [prescriptionId]
    );

    for (const item of items.rows) {
      // Fetch batch
      const batch = await client.query(
        `SELECT * FROM drug_batches
         WHERE drug_name = $1 AND quantity > 0
         ORDER BY expiry_date ASC LIMIT 1`,
        [item.drug_name]
      );

      if (!batch.rows.length) {
        throw new Error("Stock unavailable");
      }

      // Deduct stock
      await client.query(
        `UPDATE drug_batches SET quantity = quantity - $1 WHERE id = $2`,
        [item.quantity, batch.rows[0].id]
      );
    }

    await client.query(
      `INSERT INTO dispensing_records
       (id, tenant_id, prescription_id, dispensed_by, dispensed_at)
       VALUES (gen_random_uuid(), $1, $2, $3, NOW())`,
      [tenantId, prescriptionId, user.id]
    );

    await client.query(
      `UPDATE prescriptions SET status = 'DISPENSED' WHERE id = $1`,
      [prescriptionId]
    );

    await client.query("COMMIT");

  } catch (err) {
    await client.query("ROLLBACK");
    throw err;
  } finally {
    client.release();
  }
};

Billing

                                         ↓

Insurance

           ↓
        REJECTED

SUBMITTED → UNDER_REVIEW → APPROVED / PARTIAL / REJECTED → SETTLED

All tables include tenant_id UUID NOT NULL

billing_accounts

invoices

claims

8) Backend Implementation
Repository (Charges)
export const addCharge = async (tenantId: string, data: any) => {
  const client = await getTenantClient(tenantId);

  try {
    const amount = data.quantity * data.unit_price;

    const res = await client.query(
      `INSERT INTO charge_items
       (id, tenant_id, account_id, source_type, source_id, code, description,
        quantity, unit_price, amount, status, created_at)
       VALUES (gen_random_uuid(), $1,$2,$3,$4,$5,$6,$7,$8,$9,'CAPTURED',NOW())
       RETURNING *`,
      [
        tenantId,
        data.account_id,
        data.source_type,
        data.source_id,
        data.code,
        data.description,
        data.quantity,
        data.unit_price,
        amount,
      ]
    );

    return res.rows[0];
  } finally {
    client.release();
  }
};
Service (Generate Invoice)
export const generateInvoice = async (tenantId: string, accountId: string) => {
  const client = await getTenantClient(tenantId);

  try {
    const charges = await client.query(
      `SELECT * FROM charge_items WHERE account_id=$1 AND status='CAPTURED'`,
      [accountId]
    );

    const subtotal = charges.rows.reduce((s, c) => s + Number(c.amount), 0);
    const tax = subtotal * 0.1;
    const total = subtotal + tax;

    const invoice = await client.query(
      `INSERT INTO invoices
       (id, tenant_id, account_id, subtotal, tax, total, status, issued_at)
       VALUES (gen_random_uuid(), $1,$2,$3,$4,$5,'DRAFT',NOW())
       RETURNING *`,
      [tenantId, accountId, subtotal, tax, total]
    );

    return invoice.rows[0];
  } finally {
    client.release();
  }
};
Payment (Idempotent)
export const payInvoice = async (tenantId: string, invoiceId: string, key: string, data: any) => {
  const client = await getTenantClient(tenantId);

  try {
    await client.query("BEGIN");

    const existing = await client.query(
      `SELECT response FROM idempotency_keys WHERE tenant_id=$1 AND key=$2`,
      [tenantId, key]
    );

    if (existing.rows.length) {
      return existing.rows[0].response;
    }

    const payment = await client.query(
      `INSERT INTO payments
       (id, tenant_id, invoice_id, method, amount, status, paid_at)
       VALUES (gen_random_uuid(), $1,$2,$3,$4,'SUCCESS',NOW())
       RETURNING *`,
      [tenantId, invoiceId, data.method, data.amount]
    );

    await client.query(
      `INSERT INTO idempotency_keys (id, tenant_id, key, response, created_at)
       VALUES (gen_random_uuid(), $1,$2,$3,NOW())`,
      [tenantId, key, payment.rows[0]]
    );

    await client.query("COMMIT");

    return payment.rows[0];
  } catch (e) {
    await client.query("ROLLBACK");
    throw e;
  } finally {
    client.release();
  }
};
Claims
export const createClaim = async (tenantId: string, data: any) => {
  const client = await getTenantClient(tenantId);

  const res = await client.query(
    `INSERT INTO claims
     (id, tenant_id, account_id, policy_id, status, claimed_amount, created_at)
     VALUES (gen_random_uuid(), $1,$2,$3,'INITIATED',$4,NOW())
     RETURNING *`,
    [tenantId, data.account_id, data.policy_id, data.claimed_amount]
  );

  return res.rows[0];
};

  }

Evaluator
export const evaluatePolicy = (policy: any, context: any) => {
  if (!policy.conditions.role.includes(context.role)) return false;
  if (policy.conditions.device_trusted && !context.device_trusted) return false;
  return true;
};

11) Risk Scoring (ZTA Core)
const riskScore =
  (deviceTrusted ? 0 : 50) +
  (location === "hospital" ? 0 : 30);

if (riskScore > 50) {
  throw new Error("High risk access");
}
12) Middleware (Complete ZTA Enforcement)
zta.middleware.ts
export const ztaMiddleware = (action: string) => {
  return async (req: any, res: any, next: any) => {
    const user = req.user;

    // RBAC
    const allowed = await checkRBAC(user.role_id, action);
    if (!allowed) return res.status(403).send("RBAC denied");

    // ABAC
    const context = buildContext(req);

    // PBAC
    const policy = await getPolicy(action, user.tenant_id);
    const result = evaluatePolicy(policy, context);

    if (!result) return res.status(403).send("Policy denied");

    next();
  };
};

9) Security Detection Engine (Core SIEM Logic)
Example Rules
Rule 1: Multiple Failed Logins
if (failedAttempts > 5) {
  createSecurityEvent("BRUTE_FORCE", "HIGH");
}
Rule 2: Unauthorized Access
if (data.status === "FAIL" && data.action.includes("ACCESS")) {
  createSecurityEvent("UNAUTHORIZED_ACCESS", "MEDIUM");
}
Rule 3: Access Outside Hours
if (time < 6 || time > 22) {
  createSecurityEvent("ODD_HOUR_ACCESS", "LOW");
}

8) Backend Implementation
Slot Generation Logic
schedule.service.ts
export const generateSlots = async (
  tenantId: string,
  doctorId: string,
  date: string
) => {
  const client = await getTenantClient(tenantId);

  const schedule = await client.query(
    `SELECT * FROM doctor_schedules
     WHERE doctor_id=$1`,
    [doctorId]
  );

  const { start_time, end_time, slot_duration } = schedule.rows[0];

  let current = new Date(`${date}T${start_time}`);

  while (current < new Date(`${date}T${end_time}`)) {
    const end = new Date(current.getTime() + slot_duration * 60000);

    await client.query(
      `INSERT INTO slots
       (id, tenant_id, doctor_id, date, start_time, end_time, status)
       VALUES (gen_random_uuid(), $1,$2,$3,$4,$5,'AVAILABLE')`,
      [tenantId, doctorId, date, current, end]
    );

    current = end;
  }
};
Booking Appointment
export const bookAppointment = async (tenantId: string, data: any) => {
  const client = await getTenantClient(tenantId);

  try {
    await client.query("BEGIN");

    const slot = await client.query(
      `SELECT * FROM slots WHERE id=$1 FOR UPDATE`,
      [data.slot_id]
    );

    if (slot.rows[0].status !== "AVAILABLE") {
      throw new Error("Slot already booked");
    }

    const appointment = await client.query(
      `INSERT INTO appointments
       (id, tenant_id, patient_id, doctor_id, slot_id, status)
       VALUES (gen_random_uuid(), $1,$2,$3,$4,'BOOKED')
       RETURNING *`,
      [tenantId, data.patient_id, data.doctor_id, data.slot_id]
    );

    await client.query(
      `UPDATE slots SET status='BOOKED' WHERE id=$1`,
      [data.slot_id]
    );

    await client.query("COMMIT");

    return appointment.rows[0];
  } catch (err) {
    await client.query("ROLLBACK");
    throw err;
  } finally {
    client.release();
  }
};
Check-In (Queue Entry)
export const checkIn = async (tenantId: string, appointmentId: string) => {
  const client = await getTenantClient(tenantId);

  const count = await client.query(
    `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM queue WHERE tenant_id=$1`,
    [tenantId]
  );

  await client.query(
    `INSERT INTO queue
     (id, tenant_id, appointment_id, position, status)
     VALUES (gen_random_uuid(), $1,$2,$3,'WAITING')`,
    [tenantId, appointmentId, Number(count.rows[0].count) + 1]
  );

  await client.query(
    `UPDATE appointments SET status='CHECKED_IN' WHERE id=$1`,
    [appointmentId]
  );
};

Admit Patient
export const admitPatient = async (tenantId: string, data: any) => {
  const client = await getTenantClient(tenantId);

  const result = await client.query(
    `INSERT INTO admissions
     (id, tenant_id, patient_id, doctor_id, admission_date, status)
     VALUES (gen_random_uuid(), $1,$2,$3,NOW(),'ADMITTED')
     RETURNING *`,
    [tenantId, data.patient_id, data.doctor_id]
  );

  return result.rows[0];

Assign Bed (CRITICAL)
export const assignBed = async (
  tenantId: string,
  admissionId: string,
  bedId: string
) => {
  const client = await getTenantClient(tenantId);

  try {
    await client.query("BEGIN");

    const bed = await client.query(
      `SELECT * FROM beds WHERE id=$1 FOR UPDATE`,
      [bedId]
    );

    if (bed.rows[0].status !== "AVAILABLE") {
      throw new Error("Bed not available");
    }

    await client.query(
      `INSERT INTO bed_allocations
       (id, tenant_id, admission_id, bed_id, start_time)
       VALUES (gen_random_uuid(), $1,$2,$3,NOW())`,
      [tenantId, admissionId, bedId]
    );

    await client.query(
      `UPDATE beds SET status='OCCUPIED' WHERE id=$1`,
      [bedId]
    );

    await client.query("COMMIT");

  } catch (err) {
    await client.query("ROLLBACK");
    throw err;
  } finally {
    client.release();
  }
};
Transfer Patient
export const transferPatient = async (
  tenantId: string,
  admissionId: string,
  newBedId: string
) => {
  const client = await getTenantClient(tenantId);

  try {
    await client.query("BEGIN");

    const current = await client.query(
      `SELECT * FROM bed_allocations 
       WHERE admission_id=$1 AND end_time IS NULL`,
      [admissionId]
    );

    const oldBed = current.rows[0].bed_id;

    // free old bed
    await client.query(
      `UPDATE beds SET status='AVAILABLE' WHERE id=$1`,
      [oldBed]
    );

    // close old allocation
    await client.query(
      `UPDATE bed_allocations 
       SET end_time=NOW() WHERE admission_id=$1 AND end_time IS NULL`,
      [admissionId]
    );

    // assign new
    await assignBed(tenantId, admissionId, newBedId);

    await client.query(
      `INSERT INTO transfers
       (id, tenant_id, admission_id, from_bed, to_bed, transferred_at)
       VALUES (gen_random_uuid(), $1,$2,$3,$4,NOW())`,
      [tenantId, admissionId, oldBed, newBedId]
    );

    await client.query("COMMIT");

  } catch (e) {
    await client.query("ROLLBACK");
    throw e;
  } finally {
    client.release();
  }
};
Discharge Patient
export const dischargePatient = async (
  tenantId: string,
  admissionId: string
) => {
  const client = await getTenantClient(tenantId);

  try {
    await client.query("BEGIN");

    const bed = await client.query(
      `SELECT bed_id FROM bed_allocations
       WHERE admission_id=$1 AND end_time IS NULL`,
      [admissionId]
    );

    const bedId = bed.rows[0].bed_id;

    // free bed
    await client.query(
      `UPDATE beds SET status='AVAILABLE' WHERE id=$1`,
      [bedId]
    );

    // close allocation
    await client.query(
      `UPDATE bed_allocations SET end_time=NOW()
       WHERE admission_id=$1 AND end_time IS NULL`,
      [admissionId]
    );

    // update admission
    await client.query(
      `UPDATE admissions SET status='DISCHARGED' WHERE id=$1`,
      [admissionId]
    );

    // create discharge record
    await client.query(
      `INSERT INTO discharge_records
       (id, tenant_id, admission_id, discharge_date)
       VALUES (gen_random_uuid(), $1,$2,NOW())`,
      [tenantId, admissionId]
    );

    await client.query("COMMIT");

  } catch (e) {
    await client.query("ROLLBACK");
    throw e;
  } finally {
    client.release();
  }
};

4) Database Schema (Multi-Tenant)

  id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

);

  id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

5) Rule Engine (Core Logic)
Example Rule (JSON)
{
  "rule_type": "DRUG_INTERACTION",
  "conditions": {
    "drugs": ["DrugA", "DrugB"]
  },
  "action": {
    "alert": "Severe interaction detected"
  },
  "severity": "HIGH"
}
Rule Evaluator
export const evaluateRules = (rules: any[], context: any) => {
  const alerts = [];

  for (const rule of rules) {
    if (rule.rule_type === "DRUG_INTERACTION") {
      const drugs = context.prescriptions;

      const match = rule.conditions.drugs.every((d: string) =>
        drugs.includes(d)
      );

      if (match) {
        alerts.push({
          message: rule.action.alert,
          severity: rule.severity
        });
      }
    }
  }

  return alerts;
};
6) Drug Interaction Engine
Example Input
{
  "patient_id": "P1",
  "prescriptions": ["Aspirin", "Warfarin"]
}
Output
{
  "alert": "High risk bleeding interaction",
  "severity": "HIGH"
}
7) Lab Alert Engine
Example Rule
{
  "test": "glucose",
  "threshold": ">200",
  "alert": "Hyperglycemia detected"
}
Implementation
if (labResult.value > 200) {
  createAlert(...);
}
8) Risk Scoring Engine
Example (Simple Model)
export const calculateRisk = (data: any) => {
  let score = 0;

  if (data.age > 60) score += 20;
  if (data.bp > 140) score += 20;
  if (data.diabetes) score += 30;

  return score;
};
Store Result
INSERT INTO risk_scores (...)
9) Backend Implementation
Service Layer
export const runCDSS = async (tenantId: string, patientId: string, context: any) => {
  const rules = await getRules(tenantId);

  const alerts = evaluateRules(rules, context);

  for (const alert of alerts) {
    await createAlert(tenantId, patientId, alert);
  }

  return alerts;
};

export const createAlert = async (
  tenantId: string,

  alert: any
) => {
  await client.query(

     (id, tenant_id, patient_id, message, severity, status, created_at)
     VALUES (gen_random_uuid(), $1,$2,$3,$4,'ACTIVE',NOW())`,
    [tenantId, patientId, alert.message, alert.severity]
  );
};

Example Middleware
export const patientAccessCheck = (resourcePatientId: string) => {
  return (req: any, res: any, next: any) => {
    if (req.user.patient_id !== resourcePatientId) {
      return res.status(403).send("Access denied");
    }
    next();
  };

Simple: Allow / Deny

To:

Scope	Full / Partial
Duration	Time-bound
Status	Active / Revoked

consents

  resource_type VARCHAR(100), -- LAB, EHR, BILLING
  purpose VARCHAR(100), -- TREATMENT, RESEARCH
  scope VARCHAR(50), -- FULL / PARTIAL

);

6) Consent Evaluation Engine (CORE)
Decision Logic
ALLOW access IF:
1. Consent exists
2. Status = ACTIVE
3. Current time within validity
4. Purpose matches
5. Resource allowed
6. Field allowed (if granular)
Implementation
export const evaluateConsent = async (
  tenantId: string,
  patientId: string,
  userId: string,
  resource: string,
  purpose: string
) => {
  const client = await getTenantClient(tenantId);

  const consent = await client.query(
    `SELECT * FROM consents
     WHERE patient_id=$1
     AND granted_to=$2
     AND resource_type=$3
     AND purpose=$4
     AND status='ACTIVE'
     AND NOW() BETWEEN valid_from AND valid_until`,
    [patientId, userId, resource, purpose]
  );

  return consent.rows.length > 0;
};
7) Field-Level Consent Enforcement
Example
export const filterFields = async (consentId: string, data: any) => {
  const fields = await getConsentFields(consentId);

  for (const key in data) {
    if (!fields[key]) {
      delete data[key];
    }
  }

  return data;
};

export const revokeConsent = async (tenantId: string, consentId: string) => {
  const client = await getTenantClient(tenantId);

  await client.query(
    `UPDATE consents SET status='REVOKED' WHERE id=$1`,

  );

  await client.query(
    `INSERT INTO consent_revocations
     (id, tenant_id, consent_id, revoked_at)
     VALUES (gen_random_uuid(), $1,$2,NOW())`,
    [tenantId, consentId]
  );
};

export const createNotification = async (tenantId: string, data: any) => {
  const client = await getTenantClient(tenantId);

  const res = await client.query(
    `INSERT INTO notifications
     (id, tenant_id, user_id, patient_id, type, title, message, status, created_at)
     VALUES (gen_random_uuid(), $1,$2,$3,$4,$5,$6,'PENDING',NOW())
     RETURNING *`,

      tenantId,

    ]
  );

};
Dispatcher
export const dispatchNotification = async (tenantId: string, notification: any) => {
  const channels = await getUserChannels(notification.user_id);

  for (const ch of channels) {
    try {
      if (ch.channel === "IN_APP") {
        await sendInApp(notification);
      }

      if (ch.channel === "EMAIL") {
        await sendEmail(notification);
      }

      if (ch.channel === "SMS") {
        await sendSMS(notification);
      }

      await logDelivery(tenantId, notification.id, ch.channel, "SENT");

    } catch (err) {
      await logDelivery(tenantId, notification.id, ch.channel, "FAILED", err.message);
    }
  }
};

8) Backend Implementation
Get Available Beds
export const getAvailableBeds = async (tenantId: string, type: string) => {
  const client = await getTenantClient(tenantId);

  const res = await client.query(
    `SELECT b.* FROM beds b
     JOIN rooms r ON b.room_id = r.id
     JOIN wards w ON r.ward_id = w.id
     WHERE b.status='AVAILABLE' AND w.type=$1`,
    [type]
  );

  return res.rows;
};
Reserve Bed (Concurrency Safe)
export const reserveBed = async (
  tenantId: string,
  bedId: string,
  patientId: string
) => {
  const client = await getTenantClient(tenantId);

  try {
    await client.query("BEGIN");

    const bed = await client.query(
      `SELECT * FROM beds WHERE id=$1 FOR UPDATE`,
      [bedId]
    );

    if (bed.rows[0].status !== "AVAILABLE") {
      throw new Error("Bed not available");
    }

    await client.query(
      `UPDATE beds SET status='RESERVED' WHERE id=$1`,
      [bedId]
    );

    await client.query(
      `INSERT INTO bed_reservations
       (id, tenant_id, bed_id, reserved_for, reserved_until, status)
       VALUES (gen_random_uuid(), $1,$2,$3,NOW() + INTERVAL '1 hour','ACTIVE')`,
      [tenantId, bedId, patientId]
    );

    await client.query("COMMIT");

  } catch (e) {
    await client.query("ROLLBACK");
    throw e;
  } finally {
    client.release();
  }
};
Allocate Bed (Final Assignment)
export const allocateBed = async (
  tenantId: string,
  bedId: string
) => {
  const client = await getTenantClient(tenantId);

  await client.query(
    `UPDATE beds SET status='OCCUPIED' WHERE id=$1`,
    [bedId]
  );
};
Release Bed
export const releaseBed = async (tenantId: string, bedId: string) => {
  const client = await getTenantClient(tenantId);

  await client.query(
    `UPDATE beds SET status='CLEANING' WHERE id=$1`,
    [bedId]
  );
};
Mark Cleaned
export const markCleaned = async (tenantId: string, bedId: string) => {
  const client = await getTenantClient(tenantId);

  await client.query(
    `UPDATE beds 
     SET status='AVAILABLE', last_cleaned=NOW()
     WHERE id=$1`,
    [bedId]
  );
};

Register Donation

  const client = await getTenantClient(tenantId);

    `INSERT INTO donations
     (id, tenant_id, donor_id, donation_date, quantity_ml, status)

  );

Create Blood Unit
export const createBloodUnit = async (
  tenantId: string,
  donationId: string,
  bloodGroup: string
) => {
  const client = await getTenantClient(tenantId);

  await client.query(
    `INSERT INTO blood_units
     (id, tenant_id, donation_id, blood_group, component, status, expiry_date)
     VALUES (gen_random_uuid(), $1,$2,$3,'RBC','TESTING',NOW() + INTERVAL '42 days')`,
    [tenantId, donationId, bloodGroup]
  );
};
Mark Unit Safe
export const markSafe = async (tenantId: string, unitId: string) => {
  const client = await getTenantClient(tenantId);

  await client.query(
    `UPDATE blood_units SET status='AVAILABLE' WHERE id=$1`,
    [unitId]
  );
};
Compatibility Check
export const checkCompatibility = async (
  tenantId: string,
  patientGroup: string,
  unitGroup: string
) => {
  const compatible = patientGroup === unitGroup || unitGroup === "O-";

  await logCompatibility(...);

  return compatible;
};
Issue Blood
export const issueBlood = async (
  tenantId: string,
  unitId: string,
  patientId: string,
  userId: string
) => {
  const client = await getTenantClient(tenantId);

  try {
    await client.query("BEGIN");

    const unit = await client.query(
      `SELECT * FROM blood_units WHERE id=$1 FOR UPDATE`,
      [unitId]
    );

    if (unit.rows[0].status !== "AVAILABLE") {
      throw new Error("Unit not available");
    }

    await client.query(
      `UPDATE blood_units SET status='ISSUED' WHERE id=$1`,
      [unitId]
    );

    await client.query(
      `INSERT INTO transfusions
       (id, tenant_id, patient_id, blood_unit_id, issued_by, transfused_at, status)
       VALUES (gen_random_uuid(), $1,$2,$3,$4,NOW(),'COMPLETED')`,
      [tenantId, patientId, unitId, userId]
    );

    await client.query("COMMIT");

  } catch (e) {
    await client.query("ROLLBACK");
    throw e;
  } finally {
    client.release();
  }
};
9) Inventory Logic
AVAILABLE = SAFE units - RESERVED - EXPIRED
10) Critical Safety Rules
1. Expiry Check
if (expiry_date < today) {
  status = "DISCARDED";
}
2. Compatibility Enforcement
ABO + Rh matching required
3. Infectious Screening
Block unsafe units

UNIT_EXPIRED
TRANSFUSION_COMPLETED
13) Edge Cases

Rare blood shortage	alert
Expired unit	discard
Failed test	reject
Emergency transfusion	override
14) Security & Compliance
Full traceability (donor → patient)
Audit logs mandatory
No unauthorized access
Regulatory compliance (WHO, NABH)

API

ZTA Middleware
 ↓
Blood Bank Service
 ↓
PostgreSQL (RLS)
 ↓
Audit Logs

Register Donor
export const createDonor = async (tenantId: string, data: any) => {
  const client = await getTenantClient(tenantId);

  const res = await client.query(
    `INSERT INTO transplant_donors
     (id, tenant_id, name, donor_type, blood_group, consent_status, created_at)
     VALUES (gen_random_uuid(), $1,$2,$3,$4,'PENDING',NOW())
     RETURNING *`,
    [tenantId, data.name, data.donor_type, data.blood_group]
  );

  return res.rows[0];
};
Matching Engine (Core)
export const matchOrgan = async (
  tenantId: string,
  donor: any,
  recipients: any[]
) => {
  const matches = [];

  for (const r of recipients) {
    let score = 0;

    if (donor.blood_group === r.blood_group) score += 50;
    if (r.urgency_score > 80) score += 30;

    if (score > 50) {
      matches.push({ recipient: r.id, score });
    }
  }

  return matches.sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);
};
Approve Match
export const approveMatch = async (
  tenantId: string,
  matchId: string,
  userId: string,
  type: string
) => {
  const client = await getTenantClient(tenantId);

  await client.query(
    `INSERT INTO transplant_approvals
     (id, tenant_id, match_id, approved_by, approval_type, status, approved_at)
     VALUES (gen_random_uuid(), $1,$2,$3,$4,'APPROVED',NOW())`,
    [tenantId, matchId, userId, type]
  );
};
Schedule Surgery
export const scheduleSurgery = async (
  tenantId: string,
  matchId: string,
  surgeonId: string
) => {
  const client = await getTenantClient(tenantId);

  await client.query(
    `INSERT INTO transplant_surgeries
     (id, tenant_id, match_id, scheduled_at, surgeon_id, status)
     VALUES (gen_random_uuid(), $1,$2,NOW(),$3,'SCHEDULED')`,
    [tenantId, matchId, surgeonId]
  );
};

  const client = await getTenantClient(tenantId);

  await client.query(
    `INSERT INTO emergency_actions
     (id, tenant_id, case_id, action_type, description, performed_by, performed_at)
     VALUES (gen_random_uuid(), $1,$2,$3,$4,$5,NOW())`,
    [
      tenantId,
      caseId,
      data.action_type,
      data.description,
      data.user_id
    ]
  );
};
Close Case
export const closeCase = async (
  tenantId: string,
  caseId: string,
  outcome: string
) => {
  const client = await getTenantClient(tenantId);

  await client.query(
    `UPDATE emergency_cases SET status='CLOSED' WHERE id=$1`,
    [caseId]
  );

  await client.query(
    `INSERT INTO emergency_outcomes
     (id, tenant_id, case_id, outcome, recorded_at)
     VALUES (gen_random_uuid(), $1,$2,$3,NOW())`,
    [tenantId, caseId, outcome]
  );
};

4) HL7 Message Processing
Example HL7 Message
MSH|^~\&|LAB|HOSPITAL|EHR|HOSPITAL|20260415||ORU^R01|123|P|2.3
PID|1||P123||John Doe||19800101|M
OBR|1||TEST123|Glucose
OBX|1|NM|GLU||200|mg/dL
Parser Logic
export const parseHL7 = (message: string) => {
  const segments = message.split("\n");

  return segments.map(seg => {
    const fields = seg.split("|");
    return { segment: fields[0], data: fields };
  });
};
5) FHIR API Design
Base Route
/api/v1/fhir
Resources

}

HL7 → Internal
if (segment === "PID") {
  patient.id = data[3];
  patient.name = data[5];
}
FHIR → Internal
export const mapFHIRPatient = (fhir: any) => {
  return {
    id: fhir.id,
    name: fhir.name[0].given[0],
    gender: fhir.gender
  };
};
Internal → FHIR
export const toFHIRPatient = (patient: any) => {
  return {
    resourceType: "Patient",
    id: patient.id,
    name: [{ given: [patient.name] }]
  };
};

HL7 Receiver
export const handleHL7 = async (tenantId: string, message: string) => {
  const parsed = parseHL7(message);

  await logIntegration(tenantId, message);

  // route based on type
  const type = parsed[0].data[8];

  if (type.includes("ADT")) {
    await processADT(parsed);
  }
};
FHIR Controller
export const createPatientFHIR = async (req: any, res: any) => {
  const patient = mapFHIRPatient(req.body);

  const saved = await createPatient(patient);

  res.json(toFHIRPatient(saved));
};

7) Backend Implementation
Send External Request
export const sendRequest = async (
  tenantId: string,
  integrationId: string,
  payload: any
) => {
  const connector = await getConnector(integrationId);

  try {
    const response = await fetch(connector.endpoint, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: buildHeaders(connector),
      body: JSON.stringify(payload)
    });

    const data = await response.json();

    await logIntegration(tenantId, integrationId, payload, data, "SUCCESS");

    return data;

  } catch (err) {
    await logIntegration(tenantId, integrationId, payload, null, "FAILED");

    await queueRetry(integrationId, payload);

    throw err;
  }
};
Build Headers (Security)
const buildHeaders = (connector: any) => {
  if (connector.auth_type === "API_KEY") {
    return { "Authorization": `Bearer ${connector.config.api_key}` };
  }

  return {};
};
Retry Logic
export const processRetries = async () => {
  const jobs = await getPendingRetries();

  for (const job of jobs) {
    if (job.retry_count < 3) {
      await sendRequest(...);
    }
  }
};

Express Gateway Setup
import express from "express";
const app = express();

app.use(express.json());
Authentication Middleware
export const authenticate = (req: any, res: any, next: any) => {
  const token = req.headers.authorization;

  if (!token) return res.status(401).send("Unauthorized");

  const user = verifyJWT(token);
  req.user = user;

  next();
};
ZTA Policy Enforcement
export const enforcePolicy = (req: any, res: any, next: any) => {
  if (!isAllowed(req.user, req.path, req.method)) {
    return res.status(403).send("Forbidden");
  }
  next();
};
Rate Limiting
const rateLimit = require("express-rate-limit");

const limiter = rateLimit({
  windowMs: 60 * 1000,
  max: 100
});

app.use(limiter);
Dynamic Routing (Proxy)
import proxy from "http-proxy-middleware";

app.use("/api/patients", proxy({
  target: "http://localhost:3001",
  changeOrigin: true
}));

app.use("/api/billing", proxy({
  target: "http://localhost:3002",
  changeOrigin: true
}));

MODULE 26: SECRETS & KEY MANAGEMENT SERVICE

1) Core Objective
Securely manage:
API keys + Tokens + Encryption keys + Credentials

2) Architecture Overview
Services (API, Integration, DB)        ↓Secrets Manager (THIS MODULE)        ↓Encrypted Storage        ↓Access Control (IAM)        ↓Audit Logs

3) Types of Secrets
TypeExampleAPI KeysPayment gateway keyDB CredentialsDB passwordJWT SecretsSigning keyEncryption KeysAES keysOAuth TokensExternal APIs

4) Key Concepts

1. Encryption at Rest


All secrets encrypted before storage


2. Access Control


Only authorized roles can access secrets


3. Key Rotation


Periodic rotation of keys


4. Versioning


Track multiple versions of a secret



5) Database Schema (Encrypted Storage)

secrets
CREATE TABLE secrets (  id UUID PRIMARY KEY,  tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,  name VARCHAR(100),  encrypted_value TEXT,  version INT,  status VARCHAR(50), -- ACTIVE / ROTATED / REVOKED  created_at TIMESTAMP);

key_metadata
CREATE TABLE key_metadata (  id UUID PRIMARY KEY,  tenant_id UUID,  key_type VARCHAR(50), -- AES / RSA  key_identifier VARCHAR(100),  created_at TIMESTAMP);

secret_access_logs
CREATE TABLE secret_access_logs (  id UUID PRIMARY KEY,  tenant_id UUID,  secret_id UUID,  accessed_by UUID,  action VARCHAR(50),  created_at TIMESTAMP);

6) Encryption Engine (Core)

AES Encryption (Local)
import crypto from "crypto";const algorithm = "aes-256-cbc";const key = crypto.randomBytes(32);const iv = crypto.randomBytes(16);export const encrypt = (text: string) => {  const cipher = crypto.createCipheriv(algorithm, key, iv);  let encrypted = cipher.update(text, "utf8", "hex");  encrypted += cipher.final("hex");  return encrypted;};

Decryption
export const decrypt = (encrypted: string) => {  const decipher = crypto.createDecipheriv(algorithm, key, iv);  let decrypted = decipher.update(encrypted, "hex", "utf8");  decrypted += decipher.final("utf8");  return decrypted;};

7) API Design

Base Route
/api/v1/secrets

Endpoints

Create Secret
POST /secrets

Get Secret
GET /secrets/:id

Rotate Secret
POST /secrets/:id/rotate

Revoke Secret
POST /secrets/:id/revoke

8) Backend Implementation

Store Secret
export const storeSecret = async (tenantId: string, data: any) => {  const client = await getTenantClient(tenantId);  const encrypted = encrypt(data.value);  const res = await client.query(    `INSERT INTO secrets     (id, tenant_id, name, encrypted_value, version, status, created_at)     VALUES (gen_random_uuid(), $1,$2,$3,1,'ACTIVE',NOW())     RETURNING *`,    [tenantId, data.name, encrypted]  );  return res.rows[0];};

Retrieve Secret
export const getSecret = async (  tenantId: string,  secretId: string,  userId: string) => {  const client = await getTenantClient(tenantId);  const res = await client.query(    `SELECT * FROM secrets WHERE id=$1`,    [secretId]  );  const decrypted = decrypt(res.rows[0].encrypted_value);  await logAccess(tenantId, secretId, userId);  return decrypted;};

Rotate Secret
export const rotateSecret = async (  tenantId: string,  secretId: string,  newValue: string) => {  const client = await getTenantClient(tenantId);  const encrypted = encrypt(newValue);  await client.query(    `UPDATE secrets     SET encrypted_value=$1, version=version+1     WHERE id=$2`,    [encrypted, secretId]  );};

Policy

Types

Incremental	Changes since last backup

Full Backup (PostgreSQL Local)
import { exec } from "child_process";

export const runFullBackup = (tenantId: string) => {
  const file = `backup_${tenantId}_${Date.now()}.sql`;

  exec(`pg_dump mydb > ./backups/${file}`);

  return file;
};
Incremental Backup (Concept)
export const runIncremental = () => {
  // capture WAL logs or changed records
};
Scheduler
setInterval(() => {
  runFullBackup("tenant1");
}, 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
6) Restore Engine
Restore Backup
export const restoreBackup = (file: string) => {
  exec(`psql mydb < ./backups/${file}`);
};
Recovery Flow
Select Backup
   ↓
Validate
   ↓
Restore DB
   ↓
Verify Data

Endpoints

Create Backup
export const createBackup = async (tenantId: string) => {
  const file = runFullBackup(tenantId);

  await saveBackupRecord(tenantId, file);

  return file;
};
Restore Backup
export const restore = async (
  tenantId: string,
  backupId: string,
  userId: string
) => {
  const backup = await getBackup(backupId);

  restoreBackup(backup.file_path);

  await logRecovery(tenantId, backupId, userId);
};

ALLOW only:
Admin / Security roles
Immutable Backups
No overwrite allowed
Versioned backups

Replay logs
Resume service

Restore clean backup
Rotate keys

8) Runtime Config Caching
In-Memory Cache
const cache: any = {};

export const getCachedConfig = async (tenantId: string, key: string) => {
  if (cache[key]) return cache[key];

  const value = await getConfig(tenantId, key);
  cache[key] = value;

  return value;
};

export const triggerPipeline = async (pipelineId: string, userId: string) => {
  const run = await db.insert("pipeline_runs", {
    pipeline_id: pipelineId,
    status: "RUNNING",
    triggered_by: userId,
    started_at: new Date()
  });

  runPipeline(run.id);

  return run;
};
Pipeline Execution Engine
export const runPipeline = async (runId: string) => {
  await runStage(runId, "BUILD");
  await runStage(runId, "TEST");
  await runStage(runId, "SECURITY_SCAN");
  await runStage(runId, "DEPLOY");
};
Run Stage
export const runStage = async (runId: string, stageName: string) => {
  try {
    console.log(`Running ${stageName}`);
    await simulateWork();

    await saveStage(runId, stageName, "SUCCESS");

  } catch (err) {
    await saveStage(runId, stageName, "FAILED");
    throw err;
  }
};
Deployment
export const deploy = async (
  service: string,
  version: string,
  env: string
) => {
  await db.insert("deployments", {
    service_name: service,
    version,
    environment: env,
    deployed_at: new Date()
  });
};

MODULE 31: DATA RETENTION & DELETION ENGINE
1) Core Objective

Manage:

Create → Store → Retain → Archive → Delete

With:

Policy-driven lifecycle
Legal compliance
Audit traceability
2) Data Lifecycle Model
ACTIVE → RETENTION → ARCHIVED → DELETION_PENDING → DELETED
3) Data Categories
Category	Example	Retention
Clinical	EHR, prescriptions	7–10 years
Financial	Billing, claims	7 years
Logs	System logs	90 days
AI Data	Features/predictions	Configurable
4) Architecture Overview
All Modules (Data)
       ↓
Retention Engine (THIS MODULE)
       ↓
Policy Evaluator
       ↓
Archival Storage / Deletion Queue
       ↓
Audit Logs
5) Database Schema
retention_policies
CREATE TABLE retention_policies (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  tenant_id UUID,
  data_type VARCHAR(100),
  retention_days INT,
  action VARCHAR(50), -- ARCHIVE / DELETE
  created_at TIMESTAMP
);
data_records
CREATE TABLE data_records (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  tenant_id UUID,
  resource_type VARCHAR(100),
  resource_id UUID,
  created_at TIMESTAMP,
  status VARCHAR(50) -- ACTIVE / ARCHIVED / DELETED
);
deletion_queue
CREATE TABLE deletion_queue (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  tenant_id UUID,
  record_id UUID,
  scheduled_at TIMESTAMP,
  status VARCHAR(50)
);
retention_logs
CREATE TABLE retention_logs (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  tenant_id UUID,
  record_id UUID,
  action VARCHAR(50),
  performed_at TIMESTAMP
);
6) Policy Engine (Core)
Evaluate Retention
export const evaluateRetention = async (record: any, policy: any) => {
  const age = daysBetween(record.created_at, new Date());

  if (age > policy.retention_days) {
    return policy.action;
  }

  return "KEEP";
};
7) Archival Logic
Archive Data
export const archiveRecord = async (recordId: string) => {
  await db.query(
    `UPDATE data_records SET status='ARCHIVED' WHERE id=$1`,
    [recordId]
  );
};
8) Deletion Engine
Soft Delete (Phase 1)
export const markForDeletion = async (tenantId: string, recordId: string) => {
  await db.insert("deletion_queue", {
    tenant_id: tenantId,
    record_id: recordId,
    scheduled_at: new Date(),
    status: "PENDING"
  });
};
Hard Delete (Phase 2)
export const deleteRecord = async (recordId: string) => {
  await db.query(
    `DELETE FROM data_records WHERE id=$1`,
    [recordId]
  );
};
9) Scheduler
Periodic Execution
setInterval(runRetentionJob, 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
Job Logic
export const runRetentionJob = async () => {
  const records = await getAllRecords();

  for (const record of records) {
    const policy = await getPolicy(record.resource_type);

    const action = await evaluateRetention(record, policy);

    if (action === "ARCHIVE") {
      await archiveRecord(record.id);
    }

    if (action === "DELETE") {
      await markForDeletion(record.tenant_id, record.id);
    }
  }
};
10) GDPR Compliance (Critical)
Right to Erasure
export const erasePatientData = async (patientId: string) => {
  await deleteAllPatientData(patientId);
};
Data Minimization
Remove unnecessary fields
Limit retention
11) API Design
Base Route
/api/v1/retention
Endpoints
Get Policies
GET /policies
Set Policy
POST /policies
Trigger Deletion
POST /delete/:record_id
12) Audit Logging
await db.insert("retention_logs", {
  record_id,
  action: "DELETED",
  performed_at: new Date()
});
13) Security Controls
ZTA Policy
ALLOW if:
role = admin/compliance
AND tenant match
Data Protection
Ensure secure deletion (overwrite if needed)
No unauthorized deletion
14) Integration Points
All modules → data lifecycle tracking
Backup system → retain before deletion
Audit → logs
Consent → check before deletion
15) Edge Cases
Case	Handling
Legal hold	skip deletion
Active record	retain
Backup exists	verify before delete
Partial deletion	rollback

Hot → Warm → Cold → Archive
3. Secure Wipe

Data Sources
 ↓
Retention Engine
 ↓
Policy Evaluator
 ↓
Archive/Delete
 ↓
Audit Logs

Soft Delete (Phase 1)
export const markForDeletion = async (tenantId: string, recordId: string) => {
  await db.insert("deletion_queue", {
    tenant_id: tenantId,
    record_id: recordId,
    scheduled_at: new Date(),
    status: "PENDING"
  });
};
Hard Delete (Phase 2)
export const deleteRecord = async (recordId: string) => {
  await db.query(
    `DELETE FROM data_records WHERE id=$1`,
    [recordId]
  );
};
9) Scheduler
Periodic Execution
setInterval(runRetentionJob, 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
Job Logic
export const runRetentionJob = async () => {
  const records = await getAllRecords();

  for (const record of records) {
    const policy = await getPolicy(record.resource_type);

    const action = await evaluateRetention(record, policy);

    if (action === "ARCHIVE") {
      await archiveRecord(record.id);
    }

    if (action === "DELETE") {
      await markForDeletion(record.tenant_id, record.id);
    }
  }
};
10) GDPR Compliance (Critical)
Right to Erasure
export const erasePatientData = async (patientId: string) => {
  await deleteAllPatientData(patientId);
};
Data Minimization
Remove unnecessary fields
Limit retention

Surgery
Lab test
2. Payer-Based Pricing
Self-pay
Insurance
Corporate

ICU vs General ward

Emergency / Night charges

        ↓

        ↓
Tariff DB + Rules Engine
        ↓
Final Price Calculation
4) Database Schema (Multi-Tenant)
services_catalog
CREATE TABLE services_catalog (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  tenant_id UUID,
  name VARCHAR(255),
  category VARCHAR(100),
  base_price NUMERIC
);
tariffs
CREATE TABLE tariffs (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  tenant_id UUID,
  service_id UUID,
  payer_type VARCHAR(50), -- SELF / INSURANCE
  price NUMERIC,
  valid_from DATE,
  valid_to DATE
);
contracts
CREATE TABLE contracts (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  tenant_id UUID,
  payer_name VARCHAR(100),
  discount_percentage FLOAT,
  created_at TIMESTAMP
);
pricing_rules
CREATE TABLE pricing_rules (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  tenant_id UUID,
  rule_type VARCHAR(50), -- TIME / LOCATION
  condition JSONB,
  adjustment FLOAT
);
price_logs
CREATE TABLE price_logs (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  tenant_id UUID,
  service_id UUID,
  final_price NUMERIC,
  calculated_at TIMESTAMP
);
5) Pricing Engine (Core Logic)
Step-by-Step Calculation
1. Get base price
2. Apply tariff (payer-specific)
3. Apply contract discount
4. Apply dynamic rules
5. Return final price
Implementation
export const calculatePrice = async (
  tenantId: string,
  serviceId: string,
  payerType: string
) => {
  const base = await getBasePrice(serviceId);

  const tariff = await getTariff(serviceId, payerType);

  let price = tariff?.price || base;

  const contract = await getContract(payerType);
  if (contract) {
    price -= price * (contract.discount_percentage / 100);
  }

  const rules = await getPricingRules();
  for (const rule of rules) {
    price += applyRule(rule, price);
  }

  return price;
};
6) Dynamic Rule Engine
Example Rules
Night Charges
if (time > 20:00) price += 20%;
ICU Pricing
if (location === "ICU") price += 30%;
Rule Evaluator
export const applyRule = (rule: any, price: number) => {
  if (rule.rule_type === "TIME") {
    return price * rule.adjustment;
  }
  return 0;
};
7) API Design
Base Route
/api/v1/pricing
Endpoints
Get Price
GET /price?service_id=&payer=
Manage Tariffs
POST /tariffs
GET /tariffs
Manage Contracts
POST /contracts
GET /contracts
Pricing Rules
POST /rules
GET /rules
8) Backend Implementation
Get Price API
export const getPrice = async (req: any, res: any) => {
  const price = await calculatePrice(
    req.tenantId,
    req.query.service_id,
    req.query.payer
  );

  res.json({ price });
};

  return db.insert("tariffs", data);

import crypto from "crypto";

5) Evaluation Engine (Core)
Logic Flow
1. Check global flag
2. Check tenant override
3. Check role/user rules
4. Apply percentage rollout
5. Return result
Implementation
export const isFeatureEnabled = async (
  tenantId: string,
  userId: string,
  role: string,
  flagName: string
) => {
  const flag = await getFlag(flagName);

  if (!flag) return false;

  const rule = await getRule(flag.id, tenantId, role, userId);

  if (rule) {
    if (rule.rollout_percentage) {
      return hash(userId) % 100 < rule.rollout_percentage;
    }
    return rule.enabled;
  }

  return flag.is_global;
};

  return db.insert("feature_flags", {

    description,
    is_global: false,
    created_at: new Date()
  });
};
Set Rule
export const setRule = async (data: any) => {
  return db.insert("feature_flag_rules", data);
};
Evaluate Feature
export const checkFeature = async (req: any, res: any) => {
  const enabled = await isFeatureEnabled(
    req.tenantId,
    req.user.id,
    req.user.role,
    req.query.flag
  );

  res.json({ enabled });
};

PDP & PEP SYSTEM (ZERO TRUST CORE)
1) Core Objective

Enforce:

NEVER TRUST → ALWAYS VERIFY → CONTINUOUSLY VALIDATE

Split responsibilities:

Component	Role
PDP	Decides (ALLOW / DENY)
PEP	Enforces decision
2) High-Level Flow
User / Device Request
        ↓
PEP (Intercept)
        ↓
PDP (Evaluate Policies)
        ↓
Decision (ALLOW / DENY / CONDITIONAL)
        ↓
PEP (Enforce)
        ↓
Service Access / Block
3) Architecture Overview
Client / Device
      ↓
API Gateway (PEP #1)
      ↓
Service Layer (PEP #2)
      ↓
PDP Engine (THIS MODULE)
      ↓
Policy Store + Context + Risk Engine
4) PDP (Policy Decision Point)
Responsibilities
Evaluate access policies
Apply RBAC + ABAC + Context
Risk-based decisions
Return decision
Inputs to PDP
{
  "user_id": "U1",
  "role": "Doctor",
  "tenant_id": "T1",
  "resource": "PATIENT_RECORD",
  "action": "READ",
  "context": {
    "ip": "192.168.1.1",
    "time": "22:00",
    "device": "mobile"
  }
}
Output
{
  "decision": "ALLOW",
  "conditions": ["MASK_SENSITIVE_FIELDS"]
}
5) Policy Types
1. RBAC (Role-Based)
Doctor → READ_PATIENT
Nurse → READ_LIMITED
2. ABAC (Attribute-Based)
ALLOW if department = cardiology
3. Context-Based
ALLOW only during working hours
4. Risk-Based
DENY if unusual location
6) Policy Store (Database)
policies
CREATE TABLE policies (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  name VARCHAR(255),
  effect VARCHAR(10), -- ALLOW / DENY
  resource VARCHAR(100),
  action VARCHAR(50),
  conditions JSONB,
  created_at TIMESTAMP
);
policy_assignments
CREATE TABLE policy_assignments (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  policy_id UUID,
  role VARCHAR(100),
  tenant_id UUID
);
policy_logs
CREATE TABLE policy_logs (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id UUID,
  resource VARCHAR(100),
  decision VARCHAR(50),
  reason TEXT,
  created_at TIMESTAMP
);
7) PDP Engine Implementation
Evaluate Policy
export const evaluatePolicy = async (input: any) => {
  const policies = await getPolicies(input.role, input.tenant_id);

  for (const policy of policies) {
    if (matches(policy, input)) {
      return {
        decision: policy.effect,
        reason: policy.name
      };
    }
  }

  return { decision: "DENY" };
};
Condition Matching
const matches = (policy: any, input: any) => {
  if (policy.resource !== input.resource) return false;
  if (policy.action !== input.action) return false;

  if (policy.conditions?.time) {
    if (!withinTime(input.context.time, policy.conditions.time)) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  return true;
};
8) PEP (Policy Enforcement Point)
Responsibilities
Intercept request
Call PDP
Enforce decision
Apply conditions
9) PEP Locations
1. API Gateway (Primary)
All incoming requests validated
2. Microservices (Secondary)
Service-level enforcement
3. Database Layer (RLS)
Final enforcement layer
10) PEP Middleware Implementation
export const pepMiddleware = async (req: any, res: any, next: any) => {
  const decision = await evaluatePolicy({
    user_id: req.user.id,
    role: req.user.role,
    tenant_id: req.tenantId,
    resource: mapResource(req.path),
    action: req.method
  });

  if (decision.decision !== "ALLOW") {
    return res.status(403).send("Access Denied");
  }

  req.policyConditions = decision.conditions;

  next();
};
11) Conditional Enforcement
Example
if (req.policyConditions.includes("MASK_SENSITIVE_FIELDS")) {
  maskData(response);
}
12) Risk Engine (Advanced PDP Layer)
Risk Calculation
export const calculateRisk = (context: any) => {
  let risk = 0;

  if (context.ip !== trustedIP) risk += 50;
  if (context.time > 22) risk += 30;

  return risk;
};
Risk-Based Decision
if (risk > 70) return DENY;
if (risk > 40) return CONDITIONAL;
13) Integration Points
IAM → user roles
Audit → policy logs
API Gateway → enforcement
Feature Flags → dynamic access
Consent → patient-level control
14) Event System
ACCESS_REQUESTED
ACCESS_GRANTED
ACCESS_DENIED
POLICY_VIOLATION
15) Security Controls
ZTA Principles
Verify every request
Least privilege
Continuous evaluation
Multi-Layer Enforcement
PEP (Gateway)
PEP (Service)
PEP (DB RLS)
16) Edge Cases
Case	Handling
No policy	DENY
Conflicting policies	DENY > ALLOW
Missing context	restrict
High risk	block

1. Dynamic Policies
Update policies without restart
2. Policy Versioning
Track policy changes
3. Explainable Decisions
{
  "decision": "DENY",
  "reason": "Outside working hours"
}
4. Continuous Authorization
Re-evaluate during session

 ↓
PEP (API Gateway)
 ↓
PDP Engine

Policy DB + Risk Engine
 ↓
Decision
 ↓
PEP Enforcement

🔗 INTER & INTRA MODULE COMMUNICATION ARCHITECTURE
1) Core Objective

Enable:

Reliable + Secure + Scalable + Event-Driven Communication Across All Modules
2) Communication Types
1. Intra-Module (Inside Same Service)
Function calls
Internal services
Example:
Billing Service → Pricing Engine (internal call)
2. Inter-Module (Between Services)
API calls
Events (async)
Example:
EHR → Diagnostics → Pharmacy → Billing
3) Communication Patterns
1. Synchronous (Request/Response)
Service A → Service B → Response

Used for:

Real-time operations
User-facing APIs
2. Asynchronous (Event-Driven)
Service A → Event Bus → Multiple Consumers

Used for:

Notifications
Analytics
Audit logging
4) High-Level Architecture
Client
 ↓
API Gateway (PEP)
 ↓
Service Layer (Microservices)
 ↓
--------------------------------
| Sync (REST)  | Async (Events) |
--------------------------------
 ↓              ↓
Other Services   Event Bus
 ↓              ↓
Database         Consumers
5) Core Communication Components
1. API Gateway (Entry + Routing)
Central routing
Security enforcement
2. Service-to-Service Communication
REST (HTTP)
gRPC (future optimization)
3. Event Bus (Critical)
Kafka / RabbitMQ (future)
Local event emitter (current dev)
4. Message Broker Responsibilities
Decouple services
Enable scalability
Support retries

7) API-Based Integration (Synchronous)
Example: EHR → Diagnostics
await http.post("/diagnostics/orders", {
  patient_id,
  tests
});
Example: Billing → Pricing
const price = await http.get(`/pricing/price?service_id=X`);
8) Event-Driven Integration (Asynchronous)
Event Example
{
  "event": "PATIENT_ADMITTED",
  "tenant_id": "T1",
  "patient_id": "P1"
}
Publisher
emitEvent("PATIENT_ADMITTED", data);
Consumers
Bed Management → allocate bed
Billing → create account
Audit → log event
Analytics → update metrics
9) Event Catalog (System-Wide)
Clinical Events
PATIENT_CREATED
PATIENT_ADMITTED
ORDER_PLACED
LAB_RESULT_READY
PRESCRIPTION_CREATED
Financial Events
CHARGE_CAPTURED
CLAIM_SUBMITTED
PAYMENT_RECEIVED
System Events
USER_LOGIN
ACCESS_DENIED
CONFIG_UPDATED
Device Events
DEVICE_DATA_RECEIVED
CRITICAL_ALERT_TRIGGERED
10) Event Bus Implementation (Local)
import EventEmitter from "events";
const bus = new EventEmitter();

export const emitEvent = (event: string, payload: any) => {
  bus.emit(event, payload);
};

export const onEvent = (event: string, handler: any) => {
  bus.on(event, handler);
};
11) Saga Pattern (Distributed Transactions)
Problem

Multiple services → need consistency

Solution
Step 1 → Billing creates invoice
Step 2 → Insurance claim
Step 3 → Payment

If failure → rollback previous steps
Example
try {
  await createInvoice();
  await submitClaim();
} catch {
  await rollbackInvoice();
}
12) Orchestration vs Choreography
Orchestration (Central Control)
Central service controls flow
Choreography (Event-Based)
Services react to events

👉 Your system uses Hybrid (Best Practice)

1. Strong Consistency (Sync)
Billing
Payments
2. Eventual Consistency (Async)
Analytics
Notifications

Standard Request Format
{

}
Idempotency
if (request_id exists) skip();

ZTA Enforcement
Every request → PDP → validated

Correlate logs
trace_id → across all services
17) Error Handling Strategy
Case	Strategy
Service down	retry
Timeout	fallback
Event failure	retry queue
Duplicate event	idempotency
18) Retry & Resilience
Retry
retry 3 times with delay;
Circuit Breaker
if (failures > threshold) openCircuit();

/api/v1 → backward compatibility
Schema Validation
validate payload before processing;

🔐 2) IDENTITY & ACCESS CONTROL (DEEP MAPPING)
Control Coverage
Standard	Control Reference
ISO 27001	A.9 (Access Control)
GDPR	Art. 32
HIPAA	§164.312(a)
PCI DSS	Req 7, 8
HITRUST	01.a
Detailed Control Table
Control	Implementation	Module	Enforcement Layer	Evidence
User Identification	Unique user_id (UUID)	IAM	DB + API	User table
Authentication	JWT + MFA	IAM	API Gateway (PEP)	Auth logs
Authorization	RBAC + ABAC	PDP	PDP Engine	Policy logs
Least Privilege	Role scoping	IAM	PDP	Role matrix
Session Management	Token expiry	IAM	API Gateway	Session logs
Privileged Access	PAM (planned)	IAM	PDP	Admin audit logs
Deep Implementation Example
if (!isFeatureEnabled || !policy.allow) {
  denyAccess();
}
Gaps
- Privileged Access Management (PAM) session recording
- Just-In-Time (JIT) access (recommended)
🔐 3) DATA PROTECTION & PRIVACY (DEEP)
Control Coverage
Standard	Reference
GDPR	Art. 5, 17, 25, 32
DPDP	Sec 5, 6
HIPAA	§164.312
ISO 27001	A.10
Detailed Mapping
Control	Implementation	Module	Enforcement
Encryption at Rest	AES-256	Secrets Mgmt	DB layer
Encryption in Transit	TLS 1.2+	API Gateway	Network
Data Masking	Field-level masking	PDP/PEP	API response
Tokenization	Partial (needs enhancement)	Billing	DB
Data Minimization	Retention engine	Retention Module	Scheduler
Anonymization	PHI removal	Research Governance	Data pipeline
Example (Masking)
if (role !== "doctor") {
  mask(patient.ssn);
}
GDPR Rights Mapping
Right	API
Access	GET /patients/:id
Erasure	POST /retention/delete
Rectification	PATCH /patients/:id
Gaps
- Full tokenization for PCI
- Differential privacy for AI datasets

🎨 1. DESIGN PRINCIPLES (FOUNDATION)
Core Principles
1. Minimal by Default (least information)
2. Contextual Depth (expand on demand)
3. Safety First (errors prevented, not fixed)
4. Speed of Execution (≤ 2 clicks for core tasks)
5. Role Isolation (no cross-noise between roles)
6. Auditability (everything traceable)
🎨 2. DESIGN TOKENS (GLOBAL SYSTEM)
🎯 2.1 COLOR SYSTEM (SEMANTIC FIRST)
🔵 PRIMARY (Brand / Actions)
--color-primary-50:  #E6F0FF;
--color-primary-100: #B3D1FF;
--color-primary-500: #2563EB;
--color-primary-700: #1D4ED8;

Use for:

Buttons
Links
Active states
🟢 SUCCESS (Safe / Completed)
--color-success-100: #DCFCE7;
--color-success-500: #16A34A;
--color-success-700: #15803D;
🟡 WARNING (Attention)
--color-warning-100: #FEF9C3;
--color-warning-500: #EAB308;
--color-warning-700: #A16207;
🔴 ERROR / CRITICAL
--color-error-100: #FEE2E2;
--color-error-500: #DC2626;
--color-error-700: #991B1B;
⚫ NEUTRAL (UI BASE)
--color-gray-50:  #F9FAFB;
--color-gray-100: #F3F4F6;
--color-gray-300: #D1D5DB;
--color-gray-500: #6B7280;
--color-gray-900: #111827;

Avoid long paragraphs

--space-4: 16px;
--space-6: 24px;
--space-8: 32px;

--radius-lg: 16px;
--radius-xl: 24px;

--bg-secondary: #1E293B;
--text-primary: #F1F5F9;
--text-secondary: #94A3B8;

🛡️ ZERO TRUST ARCHITECTURE (ZTA) — COMPLETE SYSTEM
🎯 CORE PRINCIPLE
Never Trust → Always Verify → Continuously Validate
🧠 ZTA HIGH-LEVEL MODEL
User / Device / App
        ↓
Policy Enforcement Point (PEP)
        ↓
Policy Decision Point (PDP)
        ↓
Policy Engine + Context
        ↓
Allow / Deny / Conditional Access
        ↓
Audit + Monitoring
🧩 1. IDENTITY & ACCESS MANAGEMENT (IAM CORE)
🔐 PURPOSE

Central authority for identity, authentication, authorization, lifecycle.

🔧 MODULES
1.1 Identity Lifecycle Management
User creation (Doctor, Nurse, Patient, Admin)
Role assignment (RBAC)
Attribute assignment (ABAC)
Deactivation / suspension
Multi-tenant identity isolation
User → Role → Attributes → Policies
1.2 Authentication Engine
MFA (OTP, biometrics, hardware key)
SSO (SAML, OAuth2, OpenID Connect)
Adaptive authentication:
Device trust
Geo-location
Time-based access
1.3 Authorization Engine
RBAC (Role-based)
ABAC (Attribute-based)
PBAC (Policy-based)

Example:

IF role = Doctor AND department = Cardiology
THEN allow access to patient records
1.4 Session Management
Token-based (JWT)
Short-lived tokens
Session revocation
Concurrent session control
1.5 Privileged Access Management (PAM)
Just-in-time access
Session recording
Approval workflows
Elevated access expiry
🧠 2. POLICY DECISION POINT (PDP)
🔐 PURPOSE

Central brain deciding ALLOW / DENY / CONDITIONAL.

🔧 FEATURES
2.1 Policy Engine
Evaluate:
Identity
Device
Location
Risk score
Time
2.2 Context-Aware Decisions
Doctor + Trusted Device + Hospital Network → ALLOW
Doctor + Unknown Device → MFA REQUIRED
2.3 Risk-Based Access
Dynamic scoring:
Behavior anomalies
Failed logins
Unusual access
2.4 Policy Simulation
Test policies before deployment
🛡️ 3. POLICY ENFORCEMENT POINT (PEP)
🔐 PURPOSE

Enforce decisions at every access point.

🔧 IMPLEMENTATION
3.1 API Gateway Enforcement
Every API call validated
Token verification
Rate limiting
3.2 Microservice-Level Enforcement
Sidecar proxies (e.g., service mesh)
mTLS between services
3.3 UI-Level Enforcement
Hide unauthorized data
Disable restricted actions
3.4 Device-Level Enforcement
IoMT device authentication
Device certificates
🌐 4. NETWORK ZTA (MICRO-SEGMENTATION)
🔐 PURPOSE

No flat network—everything isolated.

🔧 FEATURES
4.1 Micro-Segmentation
EHR ↔ Billing ↔ Lab → isolated zones
4.2 Zero Trust Network Access (ZTNA)
Replace VPN
App-level access only
4.3 mTLS Everywhere
Service-to-service encryption
4.4 Network Policy Engine
Allow only required communication
